# Final report: real06

28 views: 0 polished, 28 Cycles (gate_validation 6, not_run 22). Stages: render run, gate validation not_run, polish not_run, vision check single_pass (calibration run). A polished image is final only when the gate accepted it and the vision check checked it without finding a lost or added element (§5.5). Mismatches are listed with their evidence and never auto-fixed.

## 3D files

Open in Blender: the `.blend` directly (textures packed, cameras with their metered exposure in the custom property `wenart_exposure_ev`, render settings as these images); the `.glb` with File > Import > glTF 2.0 (also other 3D tools). In the results: `final/<project>/3d/`.

| file | size |
|---|---|
| [real06.blend](3d/real06.blend) | 135.2 MB |
| [real06.glb](3d/real06.glb) | 284.3 MB |

28 cameras; textures scaled to at most 1024 px (120 scaled) for the download.

## AI decor

33 decor items chosen by the AI (Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B-FP8; both passes agreeing) in 11 rooms; 6 items by the rules. The decor stage places decor only: it moves, adds and removes no furniture (the AI completion of furnished rooms has its own section, `AI completion of furnished rooms`; `furniture/<project>/decor_report.md` has every room).

## AI completion of furnished rooms (Feature 1)

Mode `furnished_rooms: complete` (assumed: furnished_rooms, furnished_rooms_keep, furnished_rooms_keep_size, render.twin_rooms). Drawn pieces keep their anchor (+-5 cm) and front (+-1 deg); the AI may change a piece's type (within the room type's types), size, height and look (it stays `from_documents`, `modified_by_ai`, with the drawn type and size recorded) and add the pieces the room type misses (`added_by_ai`, never a second main piece).
